Unfilled TRACK = neutral grey ~(0.03,0.03,0.03)@~0.7α. **Prime suspect if the fill still won't render:** the old bar's `DefaultWhiteGrid_Low` MATERIAL fill brush (a material brush may not visually respond to `SetPercent`/`FillColorAndOpacity` like a plain image) — fallback is a plain-image fill brush, but do not pre-emptively diverge from the working castle.
- **Colors are LAW:** RED = ENEMY, BLUE = FRIENDLY (both teams), the fill tinted by team via `SetTeamColor` (data, never hardcoded in the WBP); the bar reads team by color, damage by length — never green→red.
- **Verification is LAW (unlocked desktop):** the feature is fixed ONLY when a REAL GDI SCREENSHOT of a REAL-COMBAT PIE (units actually taking damage) shows the fill VISIBLY LOWER after damage AND correctly team-tinted, and an agent CONFIRMS it in the pixels (unit + hero + tower, both teams). Prove RENDERED PIXELS, not `GetPercent()` UPROPERTY values; prove EXECUTION, not graph structure; report the observation, not the conclusion. NO "machine checks pass, ship it."
- **RETIRED by this rebuild (deleted):** `IHealthBarTarget`/`HealthBarTarget.h`, `UHealthBarComponent`/`HealthBarComponent.h+.cpp`, `UUnitHealthBarWidget`/`UnitHealthBarWidget.h+.cpp`, `/Game/UI/WBP_UnitHealthBar`. The "Overhead unit health bars (M5.5)" section below is HISTORICAL REFERENCE only.

## Overhead unit health bars (M5.5) — RETIRED 2026-07-10 (superseded by "Overhead combatant health bars — REBUILT" above; kept for history)
Added 2026-07-09 (TASK-110..112; Jonathan directive: "add a health bar to every tower and character"). Broadens GDD §7 ("Enemy hero/units show floating health bars when damaged") to **every combat actor, both teams** — see the TASKBOARD "M5.5 manager decisions" rulings block for the binding scope/behavior calls. This is a SEPARATE system from the castle bar: castles keep `WBP_CastleHealthBar` + the `FOnCastleHPChanged` delegate (M1, TASK-018/019) UNCHANGED; the M5.5 system covers units, buildings, and the hero, which carry NO HP-changed delegate (ASummonedUnit::ApplyHealing comment: "Units carry no HP-changed delegate (only ACastle does)"), so the bar POLLS the HP getters instead of binding.
- **Shared read interface:** `IHealthBarTarget` (`UHealthBarTarget` UINTERFACE, `NotBlueprintable`), header-only `Source/GitClaudeUnrealTest/Siegebound/HealthBarTarget.h` (the `TeamId.h` one-concept-header precedent). Three pure-virtual const methods (the `ITeamAgent::GetTeamId` C++-interface shape, NOT BlueprintNativeEvent): `float GetHealthCurrent() const`, `float GetHealthMax() const`, `bool IsHealthBarActorAlive() const`. Implemented by `ASummonedUnit` (→ `GetCurrentHP`/`GetMaxHP`/`!IsUnitDead`), `ABuilding` (→ `GetCurrentHP`/`GetMaxHP`/`!IsBuildingDestroyed`), and `AHeroCharacter` (→ `GetCurrentHP`/`GetMaxHP`/`!IsDead`). Team is read through the EXISTING `ITeamAgent::GetTeamId` — never duplicated onto this interface. `ACastle` does NOT implement it (it keeps its delegate bar). `AGoldNode` does NOT implement it (not damageable — no bar).
- **WidgetComponent subclass:** `UHealthBarComponent` (a `UWidgetComponent` subclass), files `HealthBarComponent.h/.cpp` in `Source/GitClaudeUnrealTest/Siegebound/`. Exactly ONE instance is added in-constructor to each of the three base classes above, named `HPBarWidget` (reuses the `<Purpose>Widget` component-name convention and mirrors `ACastle::HPBarWidget`); subclasses (AMinerUnit, ATower, ABarracks, ADeepMine) inherit it automatically. The component owns: the soft widget class, the poll timer, the show/hide (hide-at-full) logic, and the one-time team-tint push. EditDefaultsOnly tunables (so BPs can tune with no code): `TSoftClassPtr<UUserWidget> HealthBarWidgetClass` (default `/Game/UI/WBP_UnitHealthBar`, null-safe — missing asset = silent no bar, never a crash), `float PollInterval` (default 0.15), `bool bShowHealthBar` (default true — per-actor/per-BP opt-out for anything a playtest flags as clutter, e.g. miners), `float BarHeightZ` (relative Z above the actor; default 120 units — buildings/hero BPs may raise it), `FLinearColor BlueBarColor` / `RedBarColor` (default to the §6 palette / `MI_TeamColor` linear values — Blue (0.05,0.30,1.00), Red (1.00,0.10,0.05); tint is data, never hardcoded in logic). WidgetComponent space = **Screen** (the `ACastle::HPBarWidget` precedent). DrawSize small (units read at ~150 px per the card-art bar) — pin ~90×12.
- **Widget C++ base + UMG asset:** `UUnitHealthBarWidget` (`UUserWidget` subclass), files `UnitHealthBarWidget.h/.cpp`; the UMG asset `WBP_UnitHealthBar` in Content/UI/ (`/Game/UI/WBP_UnitHealthBar`) is reparented to it (the `U<Name>Widget` ↔ `WBP_<Name>` law above). Two BlueprintImplementableEvents, **float params only** (MCP cannot author enum/struct BP params — CONVENTIONS widget rule): `OnHPChanged(float CurrentHP, float MaxHP)` — WBP implements it as ProgressBar `SetPercent(CurrentHP / MaxHP)` guarding `MaxHP > 0` (identical contract to `UCastleHealthBarWidget::OnHPChanged`, so `WBP_CastleHealthBar` is the fastest donor to duplicate); and `SetTeamColor(float R, float G, float B)` — WBP tints the FILL brush from the pushed RGB. The component drives BOTH: it seeds `SetTeamColor` once at init from the owner's team, and calls `OnHPChanged` each poll while the bar is shown.
- **Behavior law (ALWAYS-VISIBLE while alive, team-tinted fill) — REVERSED 2026-07-09 by Jonathan (playtest override of the shipped hide-at-full law from commit 9a8a75f):** the bar is VISIBLE the ENTIRE time the actor is alive (and opted-in via `bShowHealthBar`), at FULL HP included, and the FILL reflects `CurrentHP / MaxHP` live — it DROPS as the actor takes damage, exactly as a health bar is expected to behave (Jonathan verbatim: *"I want the health bar to be visible the entire time … see it drop as the character takes damage … it should drop, as you typically expect with a health bar"*). It is HIDDEN only on death/destruction (the component polls `IsHealthBarActorAlive`) and when `bShowHealthBar` is false. This SUPERSEDES the original M5.5 rule (bar shown only while `CurrentHP < MaxHP − epsilon`); the `HealthBarFullEpsilon` full-HP term is RETIRED from the show/hide decision (the show-gate becomes `bShowHealthBar && bAlive`). Team tints the FILL (blue friendly / red enemy per §6), fixed regardless of HP — the bar communicates team by color and damage by length; it never recolors green→red. Applies to BOTH teams. Donor unchanged: `/Game/UI/WBP_CastleHealthBar`; never author the tree from scratch (template-donor rule). Implemented by TASK-122 (component) + TASK-123 (WBP render).
  - **Bar colors (ITEM B, added 2026-07-10, Jonathan loop-1 playtest request: *"make the part of the bar that shows the health red if they are enemies and blue if they are friendly, and the background of the bar grey"*):** the FILL is team-tinted DATA (unchanged law): `UHealthBarComponent::BlueBarColor` (0.05, 0.30, 1.00) friendly / `RedBarColor` (1.00, 0.10, 0.05) enemy, pushed once via `SetTeamColor` → the WBP's `SetFillColorAndOpacity(Bar, …)` — never hardcoded in WBP logic. The fill's `fillImage.tintColor` MUST be neutral white (1,1,1 = identity multiply) so the pushed team color shows through undimmed. The BACKGROUND / unfilled TRACK brush is a FIXED neutral GREY authored ON `WBP_UnitHealthBar` — a STATIC widget style (like the bar's DrawSize / HitTestInvisible / no-baked-text), NOT per-actor data: it never varies by team or actor, so it is deliberately NOT a runtime-pushed C++ param (a background-color param would over-engineer a constant). **Manager ruling (delegated call): the grey lives as an authored WBP brush, RECORDED here as the canonical value** — dark neutral grey ~ linear (0.03, 0.03, 0.03) at ~0.7 alpha, which MUST contrast clearly with BOTH the blue and the red fill. This closes the round-1 contrast miss: TASK-123's WBP audit found the authored `Bar` `FillColorAndOpacity` AND `fillImage.tintColor` both set to WHITE, so a draining fill was near-invisible on a white/near-white track (the likely REAL round-1 bug — the C++ may only have been harmless hardening; see TASK-122 ITEM-A QA mandate). Implemented by TASK-127.
  - **OPEN QUESTION (flagged for Jonathan 2026-07-09, NOT silently decided) — does the `bShowHealthBar` opt-out survive?** The per-BP `bShowHealthBar` EditDefaultsOnly opt-out (default `true` — suppresses clutter on a flagged type, e.g. miners) is KEPT under the reversed law. Rationale: it is ORTHOGONAL to hide-at-full — it gates WHETHER a type carries a bar at all, not WHEN a bar shows — and always-on bars make a per-type opt-out MORE valuable, not less (60+ always-visible bars > 60+ damage-only bars in on-screen clutter, §6). Manager recommendation: KEEP, default `true`. If Jonathan wants literally EVERY actor's bar always-on with no exceptions, TASK-122 can drop the opt-out — his call; the recommended path ships either way (non-blocking).

## Deck-builder & saved decks (M6)
Added 2026-07-09 (TASK-113..121; GDD §7 deck-builder screen / §4 28-card pool + per-card MaxCopies / §8 average-cost guide / §9 milestone 6). The deck-builder READS/WRITES the EXISTING deck system — it never reinvents it. Audit of the existing infra (do NOT duplicate): `UDeckComponent` (DeckComponent.h/.cpp) builds the draw pile from the `DeckCount` column of `/Game/Data/DT_Cards` in `BuildAndShuffle()`; BOTH `ASiegePlayerController` and `ASiegeBotController` own a `DeckComponent` subobject named `DeckComponent` and call `BuildAndShuffle()` at match start / `ResetDeck()` on Play Again. Today every controller gets the SAME deck (the DeckCount column). M6 ADDS a per-controller override path; the DeckCount column stays the fallback.
- **Deck data types (C++):** header-only `Source/GitClaudeUnrealTest/Siegebound/DeckTypes.h` (the `TeamId.h` one-concept-header precedent):
  - `FDeckCardEntry` (USTRUCT, BlueprintType) — `FName CardID` + `int32 Count`.
  - `FDeckList` (USTRUCT, BlueprintType) — `FString DeckName` + `TArray<FDeckCardEntry> Cards`; helper `int32 TotalCount() const` (sum of entry Counts).
  - `static constexpr int32 SiegeLegalDeckSize = 50;` — the GDD §3.4 legal deck size, referenced by the validator and `UDeckComponent`.
- **Deck legality + average-cost library:** `UDeckLibrary` (UBlueprintFunctionLibrary), `Source/.../DeckLibrary.h/.cpp` — the ONE home for deck-rule math, shared by the deck-builder widget, the DeckComponent build path, and the bot. Data-driven from DT_Cards, never hardcoded (§3.0):
  - `static bool IsDeckLegal(const UDataTable* CardTable, const FDeckList& Deck, FString& OutReason)` — true iff every entry's CardID exists in DT_Cards, every entry Count is in `[0..that row's MaxCopies]`, and `TotalCount() == SiegeLegalDeckSize` (50). `OutReason` carries the first violation for HUD/log. Null table ⇒ false + reason.
  - `static float GetDeckAverageCost(const UDataTable* CardTable, const FDeckList& Deck)` — sum(Cost×Count)/TotalCount (§8 guide — display only, no hard rule). 0 for an empty deck.
- **Player saved decks — SaveGame (cross-session persistence + cross-level handoff):** `USiegeDeckSaveGame` (USaveGame subclass), `Source/.../SiegeDeckSaveGame.h/.cpp` — `TArray<FDeckList> SavedDecks` + `FString ActiveDeckName`. Persisted with `UGameplayStatics::SaveGameToSlot/LoadGameFromSlot` to the FIXED slot name `"SiegeDecks"` (const, character-for-character), user index 0 → `Saved/SaveGames/SiegeDecks.sav`. Chosen over a DataAsset (not runtime-writable in a packaged build) and a hand-rolled `.json` (SaveGame is the idiomatic runtime-writable, cross-session, package-safe store). No file / unresolvable ⇒ empty deck list + empty ActiveDeckName (null-safe) → the match falls back to the curated DeckCount default. The SaveGame is ALSO the menu→match handoff: no deck is passed through the level-open URL.
- **DeckComponent override path (additive, backward-compatible):** `void UDeckComponent::SetPendingDeckList(const FDeckList& Deck)` sets a guarded pending list; `BuildAndShuffle()` uses the pending list when it is set AND `UDeckLibrary::IsDeckLegal` passes, else falls back to the existing DeckCount-column build (UNCHANGED behavior — the empty/illegal/unset case is exactly today's path, so nothing breaks). The pending list PERSISTS across `ResetDeck()`/Play Again (same match keeps the same deck). No new HP/gold coupling — pure card-flow, as today.
- **Curated default deck = the `DeckCount` column of `Docs/Data/cards.csv`** (re-authored in TASK-115 to a legal, intentional 50-card starter deck that SUPERSEDES the M4/M5 test spread; still `sum(DeckCount)==50`, each `DeckCount<=MaxCopies`). This single edit satisfies GDD §9-6 "replace default deck with a legal curated one." It is BOTH the deck-builder's initial/"reset to default" template AND the match fallback when no legal saved active deck exists. cards.csv stays the single source of truth (§3.0).
- **Bot decks — 2 distinct, author-controlled:** `UPROPERTY(EditDefaultsOnly, Category="Siegebound|Bot") TArray<FDeckList> BotDecks` on `ASiegeBotController` (exactly TWO distinct legal curated decks, C++ constructor defaults, tunable per-BP — a deck composition is content, not a per-card stat, so it is NOT a CSV column; mirrors the mechanic-rule-as-UPROPERTY precedent). At spawn the bot RANDOMLY picks one of the two (`FMath::RandRange`), logs the choice on `LogSiegeBot` (one line, grep-able like the §4 decision trace), and pushes it via `SetPendingDeckList`; a missing/illegal BotDecks entry ⇒ fall back to the DeckCount default (null-safe).
- **Deck-builder widget:** `UDeckBuilderWidget` (UUserWidget subclass), `Source/.../DeckBuilderWidget.h/.cpp`; UMG asset `WBP_DeckBuilder` at `/Game/UI/WBP_DeckBuilder` reparented to it (the `U<Name>Widget` ↔ `WBP_<Name>` law). The C++ base owns ALL model/logic; the WBP is layout + calls. BlueprintCallable/Pure model API (UObject/struct returns allowed on these, unlike BIEs): `AddCopy(FName CardID)`, `RemoveCopy(FName CardID)`, `GetCountOf(FName CardID) const`, `GetTotalCount() const`, `GetAverageCost() const`, `IsCurrentDeckLegal() const`, `LoadDefaultDeck()` (seed working deck from the DeckCount curated default), `SaveDeckAs(const FString& Name)`, `LoadDeck(const FString& Name)`, `GetSavedDeckNames() const` (TArray<FString>), `SetActiveDeck(const FString& Name)`. BIEs are float/int/bool/FString params ONLY (MCP cannot author enum/struct BP params — the widget rule): e.g. `OnDeckModelChanged()` (widget re-reads the getters — seed-then-bind), `OnDeckSlotCountChanged(FString CardID, int32 Count)`. Donor: duplicate an existing multi-element widget (`/Game/UI/WBP_CardHand` or `/Game/UI/WBP_MainMenu`) — never author the tree from scratch (template-donor rule). MCP CAN author full widget trees (proven TASK-041 6-slot hand, TASK-111 health bar).
- **Deck-builder card tiles = the hand's card-face composition (added 2026-07-09, Jonathan playtest request: *"physical cards displayed … same displays … for the actual match … for now"*):** the per-card tiles in the browser grid are `WBP_DeckCardTile` (`/Game/UI/WBP_DeckCardTile`, created in TASK-118). They RENDER AS ACTUAL CARDS using the SAME card-face treatment as the in-match hand (`WBP_CardHand`): the `T_CardArt_<CardID>` texture as the BACKGROUND layer, with DisplayName + Cost overlaid legibly on top ("Card artwork (hand UI)" → Face composition law). **NO shared card-face sub-widget exists to extract** — the hand's face is INLINED per-slot inside `WBP_CardHand` (driven by its `OnHandSlotUpdated` BIE + `GetCardArtTexture` resolver); per Jonathan's explicit "for now" stopgap the composition is REPRODUCED in `WBP_DeckCardTile`, NOT factored out into a shared widget (a shared-card-face extraction is deferred to a later polish pass). **No new C++:** the tile pulls everything from the EXISTING `UDeckBuilderWidget` resolvers — `GetCardArtTexture(CardID)` (art), `GetCardDisplayName(CardID)`, `GetCardCost(CardID)`, plus `GetCountOf(CardID)` / `GetCardMaxCopies(CardID)` for the counter + cap-grey — and keeps its existing +/− (`AddCopy`/`RemoveCopy`) and count wiring (TASK-118). Additive-only to `WBP_DeckCardTile`; `WBP_CardHand` and `WBP_DeckBuilder`'s grid/legality logic are NOT disturbed. Implemented by TASK-125.
  - **Above-card in-deck copy count (added 2026-07-10, Jonathan sharpened request: *"add a number above each card that shows how many you have of it in the current deck build"*):** each tile shows a PROMINENT number ABOVE the card face = the copies of that card in the IN-PROGRESS working deck. Data source is the EXISTING resolver `UDeckBuilderWidget::GetCountOf(CardID)` (confirmed to read `WorkingDeck`, the deck being edited — NOT the saved deck; DeckBuilderWidget.cpp), so this is NO new C++. The count element is the tile's existing `CountText` TextBlock (TASK-118 — today rendered as "count/max" in the bottom `HBox[− CountText +]`) REPOSITIONED above the card and restyled to the plain in-deck count, OR a new sibling TextBlock named `CopyCountText`; either way driven by `GetCountOf`. The "/MaxCopies" ratio is dropped above the card (the "+"-greys-at-cap already signals the cap). **Legibility is law, not decoration:** the count and the name/cost overlay MUST read clearly ON TOP of the card art (contrast strip / outline / drop shadow) — a number present-but-invisible against the art is the same defect class as the M5.5 white-fill-on-white-track bug. On-screen legibility + the count updating live on +/− are human WATCH items when Slate pixels can't be captured headless (never inferred from the widget tree).
- **Deck-builder navigation:** the deck-builder shows as a WIDGET OVERLAY on `L_MainMenu` (no new level, no new game mode). The EXISTING greyed "Deck Builder" button on `/Game/UI/WBP_MainMenu` (authored disabled in TASK-049) is named `Btn_DeckBuilder` — reconcile with the actual name TASK-049 gave it; ENABLE it and wire `OnClicked` to remove `WBP_MainMenu` and add `WBP_DeckBuilder` to the viewport. `WBP_DeckBuilder` carries its own `Btn_Back` (return to `WBP_MainMenu`) and `Btn_PlayWithDeck` (enabled only when `IsCurrentDeckLegal()`; sets the active deck + `ASiegeGameMode::StartMatch`). Additive-only edits to `WBP_MainMenu` — do NOT disturb the Play-vs-Bot / Btn_Sandbox bindings (TASK-072 caution).

## Deck-builder card details — click-a-card "how it works" (2026-07-23)
Added 2026-07-23 (TASK-268..272; Jonathan directive verbatim: *"lets change the deck builder interface a bit. Make it to where when you click on the display for a card it displays a detailed description on how the card works."*). EXTENDS the M6 deck-builder section above — it does not replace any of it. **Every M6 rule survives untouched:** per-card `MaxCopies` enforcement, the live x/50 counter (`TotalText`), the §8 average-cost guide (`AvgText`), deck-legal-only-at-exactly-50 (`PlayBtn` gate), save/load/active-deck, and the TASK-125 physical-card tile face.

- **ADD/REMOVE DOES NOT MOVE (manager ruling).** The tile's existing `AddBtn` "+" / `RemoveBtn` "−" (TASK-118, kept in TASK-125's bottom caption plate) stay EXACTLY where they are, still calling `UDeckBuilderWidget::AddCopy` / `RemoveCopy`, still cap-greying via `SetIsEnabled(AddBtn, GetCountOf < GetCardMaxCopies)`. There is NO whole-tile click handler today (the card art is `SelfHitTestInvisible` precisely so it never eats +/− clicks), so "click the display" is a NEW, previously-unbound gesture — not a conflict. Details-on-click is therefore purely ADDITIVE.
- **The new click target:** `Btn_CardFace` on `/Game/UI/WBP_DeckCardTile` — a transparent, style-less Button covering the card FACE (art region), inserted as the **FIRST/bottom-most child of the tile's root Overlay** so the count plate and the caption plate (which carry `+`/`−`) sit ON TOP and win hit-testing. Handler `OnCardFacePressed` → `OwnerBuilder->SelectCardForDetails(CardID)` (IsValid-guarded, the `OnAddPressed`/`OnRemovePressed` shape). It must never call `AddCopy`/`RemoveCopy`.
- **Selection model lives in C++** (the M6 "C++ base owns ALL model/logic" law). On `UDeckBuilderWidget`:
  - `UFUNCTION(BlueprintCallable) void SelectCardForDetails(FName CardID)` — stores `SelectedDetailCardID`, fires `OnCardDetailsRequested(CardID.ToString())`. An unknown/None CardID clears the selection instead (and still fires, with an empty string).
  - `UFUNCTION(BlueprintCallable) void ClearCardDetails()` — clears + fires with an empty string.
  - `UFUNCTION(BlueprintPure) FName GetSelectedDetailCardID() const`.
  - `UFUNCTION(BlueprintImplementableEvent) void OnCardDetailsRequested(const FString& CardID)` — BIE params stay float/int/bool/byte/FString ONLY (the widget rule). The WBP re-reads the getters; it never receives a struct.
  - Private `UPROPERTY(Transient) FName SelectedDetailCardID`.
- **The description is GENERATED FROM DT_Cards, never authored per card (binding ruling — anti-drift).** `UFUNCTION(BlueprintPure) FString GetCardDescription(FName CardID) const` composes a multi-line player-facing string from the row's structured fields. **NO `Description` CSV column is added, and the existing `Notes` column is NEVER surfaced to the player** — `Notes` stays "designer notes; not used by gameplay code" (it carries GDD/implementation refs like "(GDD 3.0)" / "UPROPERTY on ADeepMine" and is already PROVABLY STALE: the `Lightning` row says "in 400" while its `AoERadius` is 700). A balance pass is pending in the Standing backlog; generated text auto-survives it, authored prose would not.
  - **Composition order (omit any line whose source field is 0/None/not applicable):** (1) identity line `"<Type> · Cost <Cost> gold · Max <MaxCopies> per deck"` with Type prose Unit/Building/Economy/Spell/Hero Upgrade/Utility; (2) blank; (3) STAT block from `HP`/`Damage`(+`AoERadius` splash)/`Cadence`/`Range`(+melee vs homing-projectile per `bRanged`)/`MinRange`/`Speed`; (4) blank; (5) RULES block — one plain-English line per applicable clause, drawn from the glossary below.
  - **Glossary clauses** (each interpolates the row's OWN numbers wherever the number exists in the row): `bCharge`, `bSlayer`, `bSuicide`, `SwarmCount`, `ChainTargets`/`ChainFalloff`, spawner (`SpawnCardID`/`SpawnInterval`/`Lifetime` — resolve the spawned card's `DisplayName`, never print a raw CardID), `SpellEffect` (all five values), spell delivery (resolve `Auto` through the EXISTING `USpellLibrary::GetEffectiveDelivery` — never re-implement the per-effect default), `Profile` (Siege / Support prose; Standard omitted), and castle-damage scaling.
  - **Glossary-mirror rule:** magnitudes that are NOT CSV columns because they are mechanic rules living as UPROPERTY defaults (Charge 2 s / 2×, Slayer 150 HP / 2×, castle scaling 50 % projectile & spell / 200 % Siege, Deep Mine +2 gold/s, Masons 300 HP over 10 s, hero-upgrade magnitudes, Rally) appear as **named string constants in ONE place in `DeckBuilderWidget.cpp`**, each with a `// mirrors <Class>::<Property>` comment. Changing that gameplay value obliges updating the constant. Anything in a CSV column is read from the row and NEVER hardcoded (§3.0).
  - **Truth law (QA gate):** every rules line must correspond to behavior the SHIPPING code actually implements — the author verifies each clause against the owning class before writing it. If a clause cannot be verified, OMIT it. A description that states a rule the game does not implement is a FAIL, not a nit. No GDD section refs, no class/property names, no CSV column names in player-facing text. Target ≤ ~12 lines per card.
  - Unknown/None CardID ⇒ empty string (the WBP shows its own static hint); missing table ⇒ empty string, logged once (reuse the existing `bWarnedMissingTable` / `WarnedMissingRowIDs` spam guards). Never a crash.
- **The details view = a NON-MODAL SIDE PANEL inside `/Game/UI/WBP_DeckBuilder`** (manager ruling: not a modal, not an expanded tile — the player must be able to click card after card and keep using `+`/`−` while reading). **NO new WidgetBlueprint asset is created**: the panel is built in `WBP_DeckBuilder`'s existing runtime-constructed tree (TASK-118 technique). ⚠ **A new WidgetBlueprint may NEVER be produced by duplicate+REPARENT** — that has silently broken RUNTIME repaint in this project (design-time looked fine; ~9 wasted fixes). Build fresh or build inline.
  - Element names, EXACT: `DetailsPanel` (the panel's root container), `DetailsArtBorder` (card art, driven by the existing `GetCardArtTexture`), `DetailsNameText` (`GetCardDisplayName`), `DetailsCostText` (`GetCardCost`), `DetailsBodyText` (`GetCardDescription` — the multi-line body, `AutoWrapText` ON), `DetailsHintText` (static empty-state hint, shown when no card is selected), `Btn_DetailsClose` (clears the selection via `ClearCardDetails`).
  - The grid keeps its full card inventory: since the panel takes horizontal space, the 28-tile `WrapBox` is wrapped in a ScrollBox named `GridScroll` (closes the TASK-118 flag #3 overflow risk). Nothing else in `WBP_DeckBuilder` is disturbed — `TotalText`/`AvgText`/`SavedNamesText`/`NameInput`/`PlayBtn`/Back/Save/Load/Reset bindings stay byte-identical.
  - Legibility is law, not decoration (the M5.5 white-fill-on-white-track defect class): the body text sits on an opaque/dark backing plate, never directly on card art.
- **Verification law:** UMG render/interaction correctness here closes on a **pixel or human check**, never on property/tree readback alone — MCP readback has repeatedly passed on visually-broken UMG in this project. The strongest available MACHINE evidence for the description text itself is a headless dump of `GetCardDescription` for all 28 CardIDs (best-effort `-run=pythonscript` route); QA additionally reads the composed strings against `Docs/Data/cards.csv` by hand.

## Input-mode ownership (level-travel law)
Added 2026-07-07 (TASK-074 bugfix chain). Input-routing state set via `APlayerController::SetInputMode` lives partly on the persistent `UGameViewportClient` and can SURVIVE `UGameplayStatics::OpenLevel*` travel — **a level must never trust the input posture it inherits from whoever traveled it in.** Each level's controller establishes its own posture at startup:
- **L_Arena / `ASiegePlayerController`:** match posture is GameOnly free-look + hidden cursor (M2 TASK-023 ruling), composed by `ApplyCursorInputState()` — the only cursor owners are placement mode, the Alt-held `IA_UICursor`, and `HandleMatchEnd`'s UIOnly end screen. The controller normalizes to this posture at `BeginPlay` (TASK-074) instead of assuming engine defaults.
- **L_MainMenu / `BP_MenuGameMode`:** menu posture is UIOnly + visible cursor (TASK-049) so `WBP_MainMenu` buttons stay clickable.
- The static travel entries (`ASiegeGameMode::StartMatch` / `::StartSandboxMatch`) stay posture-agnostic — they only open the level and never set input modes.

## Dev / test tooling (non-gameplay affordances)
- **Capture lane note (MCP/editor, TASK-249):** `CaptureViewport.captureTransform` overrides work in SIE but NOT in possessed-pawn PIE — SIE is the capture lane for framed shots. Evidence: handoffs/TASK-249.md.
Names for developer/test-bench features that are NOT GDD content and NOT part of any milestone. They must not disturb the shipping flow; every one is additive.
- **Sandbox (No Bot) mode** (added 2026-07-05, TASK-071/072) — opens `L_Arena` with the full card roster but **no enemy AI**, as a calm test bench for the 22-card roster. Mechanism is a **level-open URL option**, deliberately NOT a GameInstance (no `USiegeGameInstance`, no `GameInstanceClass` config change):
  - **Option token:** `Sandbox=1`, parsed with `UGameplayStatics::HasOption(OptionsString, TEXT("Sandbox"))`. The token string is `Sandbox` — code and any future consumer must use it character-for-character.
  - **Game-mode latch:** `ASiegeGameMode` reads the option in `InitGame` into a `bool bSandboxMatch` (persists for the life of the `L_Arena` world, so `PlayAgain` stays sandbox).
  - **Bot gate:** `ASiegeGameMode::SpawnBot()` early-returns when `bSandboxMatch` is true — no `ASiegeBotController` is spawned and no Red bot `ASiegePlayerState` is created. The Red `ACastle` (`Castle_Red`) remains as a static target dummy; Blue units/buildings still march on it.
  - **Menu entry:** `static void ASiegeGameMode::StartSandboxMatch(const UObject* WorldContextObject)` — mirrors `StartMatch` but appends the `Sandbox=1` option to `OpenLevelBySoftObjectPtr`. `StartMatch` (Play vs Bot) keeps its exact signature and behavior — untouched.
  - **Generous economy:** `UPROPERTY(EditDefaultsOnly) int32 SandboxStartingGold` on `ASiegeGameMode`, default `9999` (`// dev sandbox — full roster freely playable`), granted to the Blue player once at match start when `bSandboxMatch` is true, via the existing player-state gold API (never hardcode a gold mutation that bypasses it). Normal gold rate otherwise.
  - **Menu button:** `Btn_Sandbox` on `/Game/UI/WBP_MainMenu`, label text `"Sandbox (No Bot)"`, `OnClicked → ASiegeGameMode::StartSandboxMatch`. Placed as an additive sibling next to the existing Play-vs-Bot button (which calls `StartMatch`); the existing button and its binding are not disturbed.

- **Debug exec cheats (headless-verification affordance, added 2026-07-09, TASK-121):** `USiegeCheatManager` (`UCheatManager` subclass), `Source/GitClaudeUnrealTest/Siegebound/SiegeCheatManager.h/.cpp`, set as `ASiegePlayerController::CheatClass` in the controller constructor. The engine instantiates a `UCheatManager` ONLY in non-shipping builds with cheats enabled (never in Shipping), so these commands cannot leak into a shipped game — additive and non-shipping by construction. `UFUNCTION(exec)` dev commands, each null-safe and routed through the SAME shipping code paths (never a raw field write / never a bespoke spawn): `SummonTestUnit(FString CardID, bool bRed)` (spawns a card actor for the given team via the existing spawn path — drives PIE without card input on a locked desktop), `ApplyTestDamage(float Amount)` (applies damage to the actor under the crosshair / nearest enemy through the normal TakeDamage path — induces the health-bar + castle-HP changes build-master cannot otherwise trigger headless), `AddTestGold(int32 Amount)` (tops up the Blue player through the `ASiegePlayerState` gold API). Purpose: close the recurring locked-desktop WATCH debt (TASK-076/112 doctrine — no `SendInput`, so the Blue player cannot play cards during PIE) so build-master can fully drive verification headlessly. This is a dev/test affordance, NOT GDD content and NOT part of any milestone's playable slice.

## Numbering
Variants use two digits: `SM_Rock_01`, `SM_Rock_02`

## Cross-discipline rule
The task spec's `names:` block is the single source of truth. Programmer code references and Artist asset names must BOTH come from it, character-for-character.
